- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P3, conv-gc) **S3 conversation GC on the reaper when a batch fully closes (decision 5).** New operator-side S3 client internal/objstore (New + Exists + Delete; AWS SDK v2, BaseEndpoint+UsePathStyle, default credential chain) - the operator Deployment now ALSO gets the s3SecretName creds via a conditional envFrom secretRef (chart), so the default chain picks up AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID/SECRET. reaper.go gcConversations(tasks): groups conversation-bearing Tasks (issueLifecycle/review/brainstorm) by batchID = AnnForkFromConversationKey if set else own conversation key - so a brainstorm Task (own key == its children's fork key) + all its sibling issues land in ONE batch; a solo issue/PR is its own batch. A batch's keys (every member's own key + fork key) are deleted only when EVERY member is terminal (TaskTerminal) and its LastActivityAt/Creation is older than the grace. Exists-then-Delete (idempotent; no stamping). Runs BEFORE gcTerminalTasks in ReapOrphans so conversations GC while their Tasks still carry the keys. New config S3ConversationRetention (S3_CONVERSATION_RETENTION_HOURS, default 72h, << TaskRetention 168h) + chart value s3ConversationRetentionHours. CallbackServer gained ConvStore (unexported conversationGC interface) + ConversationRetention; wired in wire.go when s3Bucket set. Metric operator_conversation_gc_total{result}. DOCUMENTED LEAK: a brainstorm batch whose siblings close >(TaskRetention-grace) apart can orphan an early sibling's key if its Task is reaped before the batch closes - small, acceptable; future fix = durable key record or SCM-based close check. aws deps added to operator go.mod. Tests: conversation_gc_test (fully-closed batch / open-sibling kept / nil-store noop / solo). gcServer name already taken in reaper_test -> helper named convGCServer.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P2, mr-review) **Dedicated review/TEST agent for all MRs incl. user-authored (decision 4).** Human PRs already routed to kind=review (mrScan); this makes that agent a real review/test agent: (1) new reviewText turn-0 prompt (turnloop.go) - review AND test the PR + REQUIRED review_verdict + never push; task_controller.go uses it for kind=="review" (else planTurnText). (2) PR head checked out READ-ONLY: candidate gained headBranch (from PRRef.HeadBranch via candidatesFromPRs); mrScan stamps AnnReviewHeadBranch on the review Task; BuildPod sets TASK_BRANCH="" (so the wrapper NEVER pushes - can't clobber the user's PR) + CHECKOUT_BRANCH=headBranch. (3) own conversation already covered (subtask 5, key pr-<n>.jsonl). WRAPPER: new CheckoutBranch (CHECKOUT_BRANCH env) -> bootstrap.Render checks out TaskBranch||CheckoutBranch; push stays gated on TaskBranch (empty for review => no push). mrScan goal reworded to "Review and test PR ...". review_verdict / POST /tasks/{t}/review flow untouched. LIMITATION (documented): checkout uses the head BRANCH via checkoutTaskBranch (branch-exists-on-remote -> fetch+checkout), so same-repo PRs work; fork PRs (head not on origin) would checkout the wrong code - needs a pull-ref fetch follow-up. Tests: pod ReviewChecksOutPRHead.../ReviewWithoutHeadBranch...; turnloop ReviewText; bootstrap ChecksOutCheckoutBranchWhenNoTaskBranch.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P2, brainstorm-fork) **Brainstorm conversation forked onto each proposed issue (decision 3).** Linkage across the lossy brainstorm->proposal->GitHub issue->issueLifecycle chain, solved operator-side: (1) proposeIssue (handlers.go) stamps annotation AnnParentConversationKey on the proposal Task = agent.ConversationKey of the project's IN-FLIGHT brainstorm Task (inflightBrainstormConversationKey: newest non-terminal Kind==brainstorm; the deterministic key is available even before the brainstorm's first turn-complete records Status). (2) maybeSetupConversationFork (lifecycle.go, called in handleTriage best-effort, gated on PodConfig.S3Bucket!="") correlates the issueLifecycle Task to its proposal by (RepositoryRef, Source.Number) and copies AnnParentConversationKey -> AnnForkFromConversationKey on the issueLifecycle Task. (3) BuildPod injects CONVERSATION_FORK_FROM_KEY from that annotation; the wrapper does the S3 copy-object fork. Two HEURISTICS (documented + flagged to maintainer via issue comment): in-flight-brainstorm assumes one brainstorm in flight per project (true today - project-level brainstorm); correlation done at TRIAGE spawn (not webhook creation) so the proposal's Source.Number is already set (avoids the open-issue race). Guards: skip if Task already has own conversation (Status.SessionID/ConversationObjectKey) or fork annotation, or no issue number. Annotations AnnParentConversationKey/AnnForkFromConversationKey in api/v1alpha1. Tests: pod BuildPod_S3ForkFromKey; controller conversation_fork_test (correlate / off-when-no-s3 / no-proposal / skip-when-owns). Chose wrapper copy-object (keeps S3 in wrapper, no operator creds) over an operator-side copy.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P1, compaction-hybrid) **Full resume vs compacted handover is now mutually exclusive, gated at 25%.** Decision 2. HandoverThresholdPercent default 50 -> 25 (kubebuilder marker on project_types.go + the in-code fallback in maybeMarkHandoverResume; CRD regenerated). annPendingHandoverResume moved to api/v1alpha1.AnnPendingHandoverResume (controller const now aliases it) so agent.BuildPod can read it without an import cycle. BuildPod now emits CONVERSATION_SESSION_ID (full replay) ONLY when Status.SessionID set AND annPendingHandoverResume!="true"; when the annotation is "true" (context >= 25%) it SKIPS the session id so the wrapper starts fresh and implementPrompt injects the compacted "## Resume from handover" text instead - never both (which would overflow the very window the threshold protects). CONVERSATION_OBJECT_KEY is still emitted while compacting so the fresh shorter session is persisted and its new sessionId recorded on the next turn-complete (natural compaction: long transcript replaced by handover-summary + regrowth). NOTE: this lowers the platform-wide handover threshold (also affects the pre-existing MRCI-failure re-entry compaction) from 50% to 25%, per the maintainer's explicit choice. Tests: BuildPod_S3CompactionSkipsFullResume; ContextGuard_DefaultThresholdIs25 (30% trips under the new default). Operator-only; no wrapper change.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P1, loop-close) **Conversation pointer recorded on turn-complete; cross-pod resume now closed.** turnCompletePayload (turncallback.go) gained sessionId + conversationObjectKey (the wrapper reports them in its turn.Record webhook body when S3 is on). handleTurnComplete calls new `recordConversation(task, sessionID, objectKey)`: RetryOnConflict reloads the Task, sets Status.SessionID (and Status.ConversationObjectKey only if reported AND currently empty - never overwrites a fork key, subtask 8), writes only when changed (no churn), non-fatal. NO stale-turn guard (unlike recordUsage) since the sessionId is stable across a conversation and carries no accumulation - latest pointer wins. PART (b) "replay on next spawn" needed NO new code: BuildPod (subtask 5) already injects CONVERSATION_SESSION_ID from Status.SessionID and the key via ConversationKey(task), so once recordConversation lands the pointer, the next-phase pod resumes. Tests: TurnComplete_RecordsConversationPointer + _NoConversationPointerWhenSessionEmpty (envtest). This replaces same-pod-only --continue with durable cross-pod resume keyed on the per-issue Task.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P1, tatara/fix-114-...) **Operator S3 conversation-persistence plumbing.** (a) Task.Status gained `ConversationObjectKey` + `SessionID` (json conversationObjectKey/sessionID) near Handover; CRD regenerated into charts/tatara-operator/crd-bases (templates/crds.yaml globs crd-bases at render time, no manual sync; deepcopy unchanged - both are plain strings). (b) chart: values.yaml s3Endpoint/s3Bucket/s3Region/s3KeyPrefix/s3ForcePathStyle/s3SecretName (all empty, cluster-agnostic rule 14) -> _helpers.tpl envConfig S3_* keys -> operator ConfigMap. (c) config.Config reads S3_* (S3_FORCE_PATH_STYLE via getBoolDefault); wire.go maps them into PodConfig (new S3Endpoint/Bucket/Region/KeyPrefix/ForcePathStyle/SecretName fields); BuildPod injects S3_*/CONVERSATION_OBJECT_KEY + AWS creds via secretEnv(S3SecretName, keys AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID/AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY) ONLY when cfg.S3Bucket!="" (feature gated off otherwise); CONVERSATION_SESSION_ID emitted only when task.Status.SessionID set (the replay, populated by subtask 6). New `agent.ConversationKey(task)`: task.Status.ConversationObjectKey override (forks, subtask 8) else <project>/<repo>/{issue|pr}-<n>.jsonl else <project>/task-<name>.jsonl. AWS secret KEY NAMES are AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID/AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY (matches rook-ceph OBC output, so s3SecretName can point straight at the OBC secret). NOTE: `make` absent in agent env; ran controller-gen via `go run sigs.k8s.io/controller-tools/cmd/controller-gen@v0.18.0` directly (CHART_CRD_DIR=crd-bases, NOT crds/ which holds the cnpg CRD). Subtask 6 wires the callback->Status write + replay.

- 2026-06-23 (issue #114 P0, tatara/fix-114-...) **Lifecycle prompts now state the phase + transient-workspace semantics.** New `lifecyclePhaseGuidance(state)` (turnloop.go) returns a "## Lifecycle phase: <state>" block: workspace is rebuilt by git clone+checkout each run, and either "file edits are discarded, only the issue/MR conversation is durable" (Triage/Conversation) or "committed+pushed changes ARE restored" (Implement/MRCI/Merge/MainCI). Injected ONLY into `lifecycleTriageText` (Triage) and `implementPrompt` (Implement) because those are the ONLY two lifecycle states that spawn an agent pod - MRCI/Merge/MainCI are operator-driven CI/merge (no pod), Conversation idles (no pod; follow-up comments route as interjections to the still-live Triage pod, so the Triage block covers it). DECISION: kept operator-side as the single source of truth; did NOT add a wrapper env/hook (subtask said "optionally") to avoid duplicating the message. The m2 equality test `buildTriagePrompt(...,nil)==lifecycleTriageText(...)` still holds since both share the helper. First step of the #114 S3-conversation-persistence plan (12 subtasks); this one has no infra dependency.

- 2026-06-23 (feat/uncap-implementation-turns) **Turn-count cap removed for implementation kinds.** `turnCap` now returns `(limit int, capped bool)`; precedence: explicit `task.Spec.MaxTurns>0` caps (override always wins, tests rely on it), else Kind in `{implement, issueLifecycle}` runs uncapped (`capped=false`, cap branch skipped at task_controller.go ~817), else `project.Spec.Agent.MaxTurnsPerTask>0`, else default 50. DECISIONS: (1) `selfImprove` is also a coding kind but stays capped by explicit user choice. (2) truly uncapped, no high backstop - per-turn stall timeout (`TurnTimeoutSeconds`) + `maxPodRecreations` remain the only runaway bounds. Live `project.MaxTurnsPerTask: 50` still caps all other kinds; no tatara-helmfile values change, just the standard operator chart+image.tag bump. Spec in docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-23-uncap-implementation-turns-design.md. Merged to main 6b5ba8e; DEPLOYED live 404d9d9 via tatara-helmfile PR #53 (3/3 operator pods Running on 404d9d9, verified).

- 2026-06-22 (feat/brainstorm-throughput O1) **Queued-autonomous budget removed; queue bounds CONCURRENCY only.** `remaining *int` param removed from `mrScan`, `issueScan`, `brainstorm`, `healthCheck`, `recoverOrphans`; budget computation block (`QueuedAutonomousCap() - queuedAutonomousCount`) and re-list recompute removed from `runScans`; all `*remaining <= 0` guards and `*remaining--` decrements deleted; `skipped_budget` metric gone. `MaxOpenTasks` and `Queue.QueuedAutonomousCap` fields deprecated in doc comment (retained for CRD backward-compat, ignored at runtime). `recoverOrphans` now always runs after `issueScan` (was gated on `remaining > 0`). healthCheck dedup key is `"healthCheck-" + proj.Name` (constant per-project, correct - no change needed). Tests updated: old `skipped_budget` / cap-truncation assertions replaced with enqueue-all assertions.

- 2026-06-20 (issue #75) **Project CRD gained `spec.agent.hooks` (lifecycle hooks) + `spec.agent.extra*` (pod-shaping).** New `LifecycleHooks` struct (preClone/postClone/conversationStart/conversationRestart/agentTurnFinished/conversationFinished, all optional command strings) on AgentSpec, plus `extraEnvs []corev1.EnvVar`, `extraEnvsFrom []corev1.EnvFromSource`, `extraVolumeMounts []corev1.VolumeMount`, `extraVolumes []corev1.Volume`, `extraSidecarContainers/extraInitContainers []corev1.Container`. DECISIONS: (1) both groups live under AgentSpec because BuildPod is agent-pod-scoped (reads project.Spec.Agent.* throughout); using upstream corev1 types keeps the schema honest (controller-gen embeds the full container/volume schemas, so the projects CRD base grew ~5.7k lines - expected). (2) BuildPod (internal/agent/pod.go) emits one `HOOK_<NAME>` env var per NON-EMPTY hook (guarded on Hooks != nil); extraEnvs are appended LAST, after every operator-set var incl. TATARA_REPOS, so a stray extra named like a required var cannot shadow it (a later dup in a Pod env list does not override an earlier one). extraEnvsFrom->container EnvFrom, extraVolumeMounts->container, extraVolumes->pod, extraSidecarContainers appended after the wrapper (wrapper stays index 0), extraInitContainers->pod InitContainers. (3) hooks are CRD-driven, NOT wrapper-chart values, so no values.yaml plain-env/list rule applies. The wrapper executes them best-effort (see tatara-claude-code-wrapper MEMORY). controller-gen v0.18.0 regenerated zz_generated.deepcopy.go + chart crd-bases. Tests in project_types_test.go/types_fields_test.go/pod_test.go. Branch tatara/task-scan-qe-fg78m.

- 2026-06-20 (feat/agent-work-queue) **Seq allocator is PER-PROJECT DURABLE (per-project ConfigMap CAS); supersedes bot PR #86 in-memory allocator.** #86 made seq per-project but kept it in-memory with a leader-gated SeqRecoverer + ErrSeqNotReady - did NOT fix the multi-replica race (webhook runs on every replica with its own map; followers collide or 503). New `queue.SeqSource{Client,Namespace}.Next(ctx,project)` stores each project's counter in ConfigMap `queue-seq-<sanitized-project>` (data key `next`), allocated via `retry.RetryOnConflict` CAS: Get/parse/+1/Update; NotFound->Create next=1 (Create AlreadyExists -> synthetic Conflict to re-Get winner); empty/missing key = 0 (->alloc 1); PRESENT-but-unparseable = hard error (not swallowed). Any replica allocates safely, no leader dep, no in-memory state. CM is UNOWNED on purpose: ProjectReconciler+RepositoryReconciler both `Owns(&corev1.ConfigMap{})`, so an owned seq CM would fire a reconcile on every CAS Update (storm); Owns filters by ownerRef so an unowned CM is ignored. Tradeoff: seq CM orphaned on Project delete (stale counter at most), no finalizer. Backoff = custom 30-step (not retry.DefaultRetry's 5) so per-project CAS contention doesn't surface spurious Conflicts. Deleted: in-memory SeqAllocator/RecoverProject/MarkReady/SeqRecoverer/ErrSeqNotReady. Re-applied on top: stale-terminal name-collision delete+requeue (admit returns requeue bool -> Reconcile RequeueAfter 1s; pool loop `continue` so siblings not abandoned), project-scoped queue gauges {project,class}, isQueued empty-state heal, reconcileDone GC-deletes Done QE, 30s at-capacity backstop, CooldownSeconds deprecate doc. Full -race suite + lint green; NOT yet merged/deployed.

- 2026-06-19 (#42) **Turn timeout is now a STALL (inactivity) deadline, not a fixed wall-clock cap.** `turnTimedOut` (turncallback.go) gained a `lastActivityRaw` arg and anchors the deadline on `max(turn-started-at, turn-last-activity-at)` + grace; both call sites (`CallbackServer.isTurnTimedOut`, `TaskReconciler.isTurnTimedOut`) pass `annTurnLastActivity`. It falls back to start-only when the activity stamp is empty/unparseable so the bound is never lost (wrapper unreachable). New annotation `AnnTurnLastActivity = tatara.dev/turn-last-activity-at`: the poll backstop OWNS it (refreshed in `PollOnce` from `TurnResult.LastActivityAt`, which `GetTurn`/http.go now deserializes from the wrapper's `lastActivityAt`); the reconcile path only READS it (same shape as turn-started-at). Freshness is bounded by `pollRequeue`=30s / `pollGetTurnTimeout`=5s, fine for a minute-scale window. The stamp is cleared everywhere the other turn annotations are reset (recordTurn new-turn, expireTimedOutTurn, lifecycle reset, webhook reactivation) so it never leaks into the next turn. `turnTimeoutSeconds` field name kept (CRD contract); its doc comment + `operator_turn_timeout_total` Help now say "stall/inactivity". This is the COMPANION to the wrapper change (tatara-claude-code-wrapper #42): the wrapper enforces its own `AfterFunc(TurnTimeout)` reset-on-activity and exposes `lastActivityAt`; the wrapper timer fires first (operator backstop is +60s on the same TURN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS), so the operator change alone would not have fixed it - but without the operator change the +60s backstop would re-kill the productive turns the wrapper now protects.

- 2026-06-16 (fix/audit-2026-06-15, review) **Callback HMAC secret delivered via SecretKeyRef + chart-wired.** Review fix: the r3 HMAC fix injected CALLBACK_HMAC_SECRET into wrapper Pods as a literal env Value (leaked the shared secret into every Pod spec/etcd, contradicting the secretEnv pattern used for every other agent secret and hard rule 6) AND had NO chart wiring (no values key / Secret / envFrom), so the security feature was entirely dormant - the "trust gap closed" claim was false in any real deploy. Fix: new `callbackHmacSecretName`/`callbackHmacSecret` values render a managed Secret (key `callback-hmac-secret`); operator reads CALLBACK_HMAC_SECRET via SecretKeyRef (deployment env) for verification, wrapper Pods reference it via SecretKeyRef (CALLBACK_HMAC_SECRET_NAME). Empty = HMAC disabled, NetworkPolicy remains sole control. Wrapper-side signing of X-Tatara-Signature is still a tatara-claude-code-wrapper change, gate before enabling.

- 2026-06-16 (fix/audit-2026-06-15) **Metrics server unauthenticated /metrics - INTENTIONAL, NetworkPolicy is the sole control.** `metricsserver.Options` binds plain HTTP on `:9090` with no RBAC filter (`filters.WithAuthenticationAndAuthorization` not set). Threat model: /metrics is an internal-only bind, protected by cluster NetworkPolicy limiting access to the Prometheus scraper Service Account. Adding kube-rbac-proxy or the controller-runtime auth filter would require additional RBAC bindings and a sidecar or TLS cert, which adds complexity disproportionate to the risk on a pod-network-only endpoint. Decision reviewed and accepted per hard rule 4. Revisit if the operator gains an external-facing ingress or the NetworkPolicy is relaxed.

- 2026-06-16 (fix/audit-2026-06-15) **Empty agentScheduling = unconstrained pod placement.** The agent package and chart are correct (cluster-specific scheduling is helmfile-supplied per rule 14). An empty agentScheduling (the chart default) means no nodeSelector/tolerations/affinity on spawned wrapper pods - they can land on control-plane nodes. The tatara-helmfile MUST set agentScheduling with a nodeSelector or toleration omission to repel control-plane taints. See MEMORY global: agent-pods-unbounded-control-plane.

- 2026-06-16 (fix/audit-2026-06-15) **Webhook audit fixes (7 findings).** Fixes applied to `internal/webhook/server.go`: (1) HIGH panic on GitLab MR IssueRef - `strings.LastIndex("#")` returned -1 for `group/proj!N`; replaced with `strings.LastIndexAny("#!")` via new `issueRefRepoSlug` helper (3 sites). (2) MEDIUM TOCTOU duplicate create - `GenerateName` let concurrent deliveries both succeed; switched issueLifecycle to deterministic `issueLifecycleTaskName` (SHA-256 of projectName+issueRef), treat `AlreadyExists` as duplicate. (3) MEDIUM empty webhookSecret accepted - added `len(v)==0` guard (same path as missing-key). (4) MEDIUM GitLab MR kind misclassified as issueLifecycle when bot is actor - restricted `AuthorLogin==bot` branch to `provider=="github"` only (GitLab actor!=author; authoritative gate stays in GetPRState per scm-author-vs-actor-egress-gate). (5) LOW replay protection absent - ACCEPTED RISK, documented here per hard rule 4; no delivery-id dedup, window attack feasible for issue_comment re-queue/re-activate; mitigated by idempotency of most paths; full dedup needs in-memory LRU or ConfigMap store (out of scope this cycle). (6) LOW label-unfiltered List - added `MatchingLabels` to dedup query in `handleWorkItem` and to `findLifecycleTask`/`findReactivatableTask` via `taskListOpts`. (7) LOW nil Metrics - panic in `NewServer` with clear message if `cfg.Metrics==nil` (consistent with explicit requirement contract, mirrors how nil Logger is handled via a default). All covered by new tests in `audit_fixes_test.go`.

Past decisions and their context. One line per entry, dated. Append-only
in spirit; prune only when a decision is reversed.

- 2026-06-15 (feat/project-level-brainstorm) **Brainstorm is now PROJECT-LEVEL (one Task per project per cycle).** Old: one brainstorm Task per repo per cycle (O(#repos) creates). New: one Task per cycle for the whole project. Three guards: (1) project-scoped in-flight: ANY non-terminal brainstorm Task blocks (`brainstormInFlightProject`, drops RepositoryRef clause); (2) summed backlog across repos with short-circuit once total >= maxProp (avoids extra SCM calls); (3) shared budget as before. Primary repo for pod/branch stamping: deterministic sort by Task CR name (alphabetically first valid slug). Goal text changed: `brainstormGoalProject(slugs)` instructs agent to survey ALL repos (available via TATARA_REPOS in the pod) and call `propose_issue` with the chosen target `repo` arg. Old `brainstormGoal(slug)` kept as compat wrapper -> delegates to `brainstormGoalProject`. CRD: `MaxOpenProposals` default bumped 3->5, doc updated to "across ALL repos in the project". CRD manifests regenerated. `brainstormInFlight(existing, repoName)` per-repo helper removed (was unused after the refactor). PR: feat/project-level-brainstorm.

- 2026-06-14 (#29) **tatara_lifecycle_state gauge recomputed from cluster state (no more deltas).** The gauge was maintained ONLY by `AddLifecycleState(from,-1)/+(to,1)` deltas in `setLifecycleState` (lifecycle.go), so every operator restart (set-image deploy, replicas=1) reset it to 0 and the first post-restart transition drove the `from` series NEGATIVE, while GC'd terminal Tasks (e.g. the MaxOpenTasks 40-Task purge) left `Done/Stopped/Parked` monotonically climbing. FIX (mirrors the `updateMemoryStackCounts`/`SetMemoryStackCounts` precedent the team already adopted for `operator_memory_stacks`): new `ProjectReconciler.updateLifecycleStateCounts` lists every Task, counts `Status.LifecycleState` for `Kind=="issueLifecycle"`, and `Set`s ALL 9 known states (`lifecycleStates` var: Triage/Implement/Conversation/MRCI/Merge/MainCI/Done/Stopped/Parked) including 0 for drained states, so an emptied state reads 0 not its last value. Called from `Reconcile` right after `updateMemoryStackCounts` (hourly + 60s backlogRequeue cadence; runs regardless of cron config, unlike runScans which early-returns on nil Scm/Cron/ReaderFor). Maintainer go-ahead on all 3 open Qs: (1) home in ProjectReconciler next to the precedent, (2) DROP the per-transition deltas entirely - the absolute `Set` is the single source of truth, staleness bounded to <=60s, and `tatara_lifecycle_transition_total` already gives per-event resolution, (3) explicit `Kind` filter so no `state=""` series. Dead `AddLifecycleState` method removed (no callers left). `LifecycleMetrics` field added to ProjectReconciler + wired in wire.go. TDD: TestUpdateLifecycleStateCounts_RecomputeCorrectsSkewedGauge skews a state high, one negative, one terminal, asserts recompute converges every state to truth (zeros included) and a non-issueLifecycle Task in a lifecycle state is NOT counted. Full `go test ./...` green.
- 2026-06-14 **Push-metrics receiver for short-lived wrapper pods (tatara-operator#42 B1).** New `internal/pushmetrics` package: a `Receiver` that is both an HTTP push endpoint and an unchecked `prometheus.Collector`. Wrappers POST their /metrics text to `POST /internal/metrics/push?run_id=&pod=&job=` on the INTERNAL_ADDR listener (not ingress, no OIDC, mounted alongside the turn-complete callback in `turncallback.go`); the receiver parses the text (`expfmt.NewTextParser(model.UTF8Validation)` - the zero-value TextParser PANICS in common v0.67 because NameValidationScheme is unset), force-stamps run_id/pod/job identity labels on every series (so a run can't spoof another's identity), and keys the snapshot by run_id. `Collect` re-exposes all live runs on the operator's own /metrics (registered on `ctrlmetrics.Registry` in main.go) and is union-label-padded per metric name so two runs pushing the same metric with different label sets gather cleanly instead of erroring the whole scrape; `Describe` sends NOTHING (required to register as an unchecked collector that may emit arbitrary wrapper-chosen descriptors). TTL eviction (config `PUSH_METRICS_TTL`, default 5m) ages out a hard-killed pod's series at Collect time; DELETE on the same path is the wrapper's best-effort graceful cleanup. Self-metrics: `operator_pushed_runs` gauge, `operator_push_receive_total{result}`, `operator_pushed_runs_evicted_total`. Deliberately NOT the upstream Pushgateway (no expiry = silent-staleness footgun). The human approved the issue's proposed-default design with "go". REMAINING (subtasks, separate PRs): B2 wrapper-side push client in tatara-claude-code-wrapper, B3 per-repo instrumentation gaps for the long-lived services. TDD, gofmt/lint/`go test ./...` all green.
- 2026-06-14 **Operator instrumentation gaps filled (tatara-operator#42 B3, operator PR).** Fleet-audit found the operator's happy-path was well-metered but several silent failure/timeout paths in background reconcile/scan/callback loops had NO metric (they never surface as HTTP errors). Added three counters to OperatorMetrics (obs/operator_metrics.go): `operator_turn_timeout_total{source}` (source=reconcile|poll_backstop) wired at task_controller.go isTurnTimedOut and turncallback.go poll backstop; `operator_ingest_job_total{result}` (success|failure) wired at repository_controller.go handleFinishedJob both branches (pairs with the existing duration histogram which only fires on success); `operator_agent_unreachable_termination_total` wired at task_controller.go handleAgentUnreachable boot-deadline termination. Label combos pre-initialised. obs unit tests added; controller envtest suite + lint + gofmt green. AUDIT FOLLOW-UPS (not done, lower value / spread across many call sites - candidates for a later PR): REST API handler outcome counter, agent SubmitTurn/Interject error counters, scan-loop SCM read-error counter, memory-provision failure counter, conflict-retry counter, wrapper pod/svc cleanup counter. tatara-chat audit: HTTP+domain metrics already solid; remaining gaps are store-layer durations whose failures already surface as HTTP 500s (lower value) - separate subtask. tatara-memory-repo-ingester: ZERO metrics but it is a run-to-completion Job (pull-scraping not viable) - needs a push-vs-pull design decision (issue says pull, but a Job can't be reliably scraped; B1 push receiver is the natural fit) - separate subtask, NOT self-implemented. tatara-memory: confirmed complete baseline, no change.
- 2026-06-14 **Wrapper push-metrics env wiring (tatara-operator#42 B2, operator side).** `BuildPod` (internal/agent/pod.go) now sets three env vars so the short-lived wrapper Pod can push its /metrics to the B1 receiver: `OPERATOR_PUSH_URL` = `TrimSuffix(cfg.CallbackURL,"/") + "/internal/metrics/push"` (same internal listener as the turn-complete callback), and `RUN_ID` = `POD_NAME` = `PodName(task)` (the wrapper Pod name; one pod per run, so it is the run identity - successive runs of a task reuse the name but never run concurrently, and the old pod's series are DELETE'd on graceful exit or TTL'd otherwise). The wrapper-side client lives in tatara-claude-code-wrapper (`internal/pushclient`).
- 2026-06-14 **mise toolchain rollout (chore/mise-toolchain).** .mise.toml added (go 1.26.3, golangci-lint 2.12.2, helm 4.2.1; min_version 2026.6.3; tasks lint/test/build -> make). CI lint/test/build jobs replaced setup-go + golangci-lint-action with jdx/mise-action@v2. controller-gen/envtest left as go-run-at-version in Makefile (out of scope; pinned versions, no value in mise-managing them). `make test` runs `go test -race` which requires gcc; test job keeps a `build-essential` apt step before mise-action (mise does NOT provide gcc). Same step kept in lint/build jobs to match origin/main baseline.

- 2026-06-14 **Issue/MR comments interrupt the agent nursing them (tatara-operator#25).** New `Status.PendingInterjections []string`. Webhook `handleIssueComment` (server.go) now also routes MR comments (intercept on `ev.Action=="created"`, dropping the `!IsPR` gate): when the live task has a turn in flight (`taskHasInflightTurn`: current-turn set, turn-complete unset) it queues the COMMENT text (new `WebhookEvent.CommentBody`, surfaced from GitHub `comment.body` / GitLab note `object_attributes.note`) onto PendingInterjections (capped 20) instead of bumping the deadline; idle tasks keep the re-triage path; no-live-task now reactivates Parked OR creates a fresh Triage task for MRs too. Reconciler drains PendingInterjections at the top of `reconcileLifecycle` (mirrors PendingComments): in-flight -> `Session.Interject` each -> wrapper `POST /v1/interject` -> live PTY; UnreachableError retains+retries, no in-flight turn drops the stale queue. All TDD, lint+test green.

- 2026-06-14 **Brainstorm follow-up no longer interrogates; brainstorm yield is honest.** Two fixes shipped with the starvation fix. (1) FOLLOW-UP SILENCE: the issueLifecycle discuss arm (`finishTriage`, lifecycle.go) now gates `triagePostComment` on `tataraAuthoredIssue && hasHumanComment` - a bot-authored issue with no human reply gets NO repeated "still awaiting go-ahead" comment (live bug: tatara-operator#29 got 3 near-identical re-asks ~1h apart). `lifecycleTriageText` (turnloop.go) prompt softened to emit `action=discuss` with empty comment + NOT use the comment tool when no human replied. Fail-open on check errors; human-replied + human-filed paths still always comment. The silently-held task idles to the 60min Conversation deadline -> Stopped (no loop). (2) PROPOSE HONESTY: `brainstormGoal` (projectscan.go) now mandates `propose_issue` + single-decision framing (no open-questions list); writeback (writeback.go) sets reason `BrainstormProposed` ONLY when `brainstormHasProposal` finds a proposal child Task (spec.proposedIssue + same project/repo) created at/after THIS brainstorm run (creation-time scoped, since proposals carry no per-run linkage), else neutral `BrainstormComplete` - so the >50% no-yield rate is no longer masked. All TDD, opus-reviewed (1 medium = per-run scoping, fixed), make test green. NOTE: the deep-research SKILL itself is still unbuilt (designed only) - the goal names it but the wrapper image ships no such skill; building it is the remaining depth fix.
- 2026-06-14 **Brainstorm starvation FIXED (option A): Conversation excluded from creation budget.** `taskOpen()` now returns false for `LifecycleState=="Conversation"` (awaiting-human). Previously 3 Conversation issueLifecycle tasks (maxOpenTasks=3) drove budget=0 every hourly fire -> 0 new brainstorm tasks in 6h. Conversation is externally gated so it must not consume an autonomous concurrency slot. TaskActive (concurrency cap, fix #30) is unchanged - that uses a separate predicate. TDD: TestTaskOpen_TerminalPhases added Conversation cases, TestOpenTaskCount_ConversationExcluded validates the starvation scenario end-to-end. All green make test.
- 2026-06-14 **Concurrency-cap deadlock FIXED (live incident: operator stopped processing).** `atConcurrencyCap` (task_controller.go) counted tasks toward the per-Project cap by `isActive(phase)` (Planning/Running) only, NOT excluding terminal lifecycleState - unlike `taskOpen`/`laneOccupancy`, which both use `isLifecycleTerminal` (Done/Stopped/Parked). When a Task gives up at maxIterations, `setLifecycleState` flips LifecycleState=Parked but LEAVES Status.Phase=="Planning" (+ leaves the wrapper pod running). So 3 Parked-but-Planning zombies (scan-6g447, task-h8p6d, task-nvzr2) permanently counted as 3 active -> every new task "gated at concurrency cap" forever -> operator processed nothing. FIX: new `taskActive(t)=isActive(phase) && !isLifecycleTerminal(lc)`; `atConcurrencyCap` + `updateInflightGauge` (boy-scout: same latent phase-only bug, was over-counting the inflight gauge) both use it. TDD (TestTaskActive_ExcludesTerminalLifecycle), opus-reviewed, make test green. Operationally unblocked live by patching the 3 zombies `phase=Failed` + deleting their idle pods (cap freed, processing resumed). FOLLOW-UP (NOT fixed): a Park/terminal transition does NOT tear down the wrapper Pod+Service -> idle zombie pods leak (had to manually delete; also seen for Succeeded tasks). With the cap fix they no longer deadlock, but terminal-lifecycle should delete pod+svc. SEPARATE same-incident regression (fixed elsewhere): the wrapper agent.image was bumped to the CHART tag `0.0.0-468668a`; container tag is bare `468668a` -> agents ImagePullBackOff'd (tatara-helmfile PR #3 corrected it).
- 2026-06-13 (deployed 306f596, set-image) **Phase-label dedup + orphan-recovery (presence+state, Option A).** Fixes duplicate-assignment + orphaned-MR. Four operator-managed phase labels (tatara-brainstorming/-approved/-implementation/-declined) = issue state-of-truth, set at lifecycle state ENTRY (brainstorming on Triage, approved on finishTriage-implement, implementation on Implement entry, declined on triage-close; issue sources only, skip PRs - ensurePhaseLabel in lifecycle.go). Dedup + backstop key on label PRESENCE + Task STATE, NOT label-added-time: the approved spec's label-time-vs-task-creation rule (+ new IssueLabelAddedAt) was WITHDRAWN because a Task always predates the labels it sets, so it re-spawned a duplicate triage on every orphaned terminal Task (see [[phase-label-dedup-presence-not-time]]). isDeduped(c,existing,managed) issue arm: non-terminal Task=active (fast path unchanged); only-terminal + any managed label present=orphan->dedup; no managed label->fallback updatedAt-vs-creation. Backstop recoverOrphans (runScans after issueScan, gated budget>0): re-lists Tasks (sees same-cycle mrScan MRCI tasks for open MRs so an open bot MR is not double-recovered), per OPEN issue with an active label + no live Task starts the right entry (implementation/approved->Implement, brainstorming/idea->Triage, declined->skip), MaxOpenTasks-bounded. Webhook handleIssueComment: a human comment on an issue whose only owning Task is Parked REACTIVATES it (clean reset: Phase="", 5 turn annotations cleared via shared api/v1alpha1.AnnCurrentTurn consts, pod+svc deleted) instead of duplicating; Done/none create fresh. Lazy legacy migration: managedPhaseLabels includes tatara-idea/-rejected so setLifecycleLabel strips them on next egress + dedup/backstop treat legacy idea as active - no migration job. Built via Workflow (5-task sequential subagent-driven TDD: sonnet impl + opus review per task + final review); all green (go test ./..., golangci-lint 0). Merged operator main 306f596 (clean auto-merge over bot #24/#26). Deployed push->CI->set-image (helm clean-apply STILL blocked on tatara-scm SSA token). Spec+plan in parent tatara repo docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-13-tatara-phase-label-dedup-design.md AMENDMENT, plans/2026-06-13-tatara-phase-label-dedup.md}. Live: pod 306f596 healthy 0-restart, cap+lifecycle working, zero errors; backstop gated while at cap=3, so label/backstop behavior verifies on the next hourly issueScan once budget frees. KNOWN cosmetic: proposalBacklog doc comment still says "idea label" (function counts brainstorming+legacy idea) - optional cleanup.
- 2026-06-13 (deployed d045a9d) **Issue close is now gated: never close an issue that has an unmerged change.** Bug: tatara closed issues whose MR was unmerged/conflicting + main CI not green. Root cause (workflow-investigated): two AGENT-driven close paths fired writer.CloseIssue with zero merge/CI guard - finishTriage close arm -> triageCloseIssue (lifecycle.go) and writeBackIssue close arm (writeback.go, triageIssue kind), both from the agent's issue_outcome=close. The demonstrated incident is the issueLifecycle RE-TRIAGE path: handleIssueComment resets a task that already has an open/conflicting MR back to Triage; the agent re-triages and emits close; the old code closed the issue, orphaning the unmerged MR. The ONLY correct close is handleMainCI (MergeCommitSHA recorded on a successful Merge + that commit's main CI == "success"). FIX: hasUnmergedChange(task) = PrURL!="" || HeadBranch!=""; both agent-close paths now WITHHOLD the close when it's true (finishTriage routes to Conversation as tatara-idea + posts a "not closing - unmerged change" comment; writeBackIssue clears writeback with CloseWithheldUnmerged); triageCloseIssue also has a defensive guard. A pure triage REJECT (no code artifact) still closes + labels tatara-rejected - that is not an unmerged-code case. handleMainCI (the merged+green close) is unchanged. NOTE (not fixed, low-prob): handleMainCI trusts MergeCommitSHA + the commit's main-CI status without re-checking PR.merged_at; a merge that returned a SHA but didn't persist could still close - hardening would add a GetPRState merged check.
- 2026-06-13 (deployed d045a9d) **propose_issue RepositoryRef slug bug FIXED; brainstorm re-enabled.** createProposal now calls resolveRepository(namespace, projectRef, ref): tries the CR name (only if ref has no "/", else the API server rejects "owner/repo" as an invalid name - this was a test-caught trap), then matches the ref against each Project Repository by owner/repo of its URL. So the agent may pass either "tatara-cli" or "owner/tatara-cli". Re-enabled brainstorm on Project tatara (was disabled during the runaway). Verified live: the "Repository ... not found" createProposal error-loop is gone; open Tasks hold at the MaxOpenTasks cap (3). Both this and the close-guard deployed via buildx -> set-image (helm clean apply still blocked on the tatara-scm token SSA conflict - see prior entry).

- 2026-06-13 (deployed 4dffcc2) **MaxOpenTasks hard cap (default 3) on AUTONOMOUS task creation.** Trigger incident: deploying the 3-label lifecycle (which REMOVED the ApprovalRequired gate) unparked 40 proposal Tasks that had been safely held at AwaitingApproval, freeing them to error-loop/self-implement. Root flood cause: brainstorm kept minting proposals AND `propose_issue`/createProposal stored the repo SLUG `szymonrychu/<repo>` in `ProposedIssue.RepositoryRef` instead of the Repository CR name `<repo>` -> createProposal's `r.Get(Name: RepositoryRef)` 404s ("Repository ... not found") -> issue never opens -> the issue-count backpressure (proposalBacklog) never trips -> unbounded proposal Tasks. Containment: scaled operator deploy to 0 (reversible freeze), deleted the 40 AwaitingApproval proposal Tasks (their SCM issues persist + re-triage as tatara-idea). Fix: new `ProjectSpec.MaxOpenTasks` (kubebuilder default 3); `runScans` computes `budget = MaxOpenTasks - openTaskCount(existing)` and threads a SHARED `*budget` through mrScan/issueScan/brainstorm; each breaks at `*budget<=0` and decrements only on successful create -> total autonomous creations hard-capped. `maxOpenTasks()` defaults to 3 IN CODE, so set-image alone enforces it even before the CRD field reaches the cluster. Webhook is EXEMPT (human-initiated, must not silently drop). taskOpen() = not Succeeded/Failed phase AND lifecycleState not Done/Stopped/Parked. VERIFIED live: log "scan: at open-task cap; skipping autonomous creation cap=3", open Tasks held at 3. brainstorm DISABLED on Project tatara (kubectl patch spec.scm.cron.brainstorm.enabled=false) until the RepositoryRef bug is fixed (else it mints failing proposals that consume the budget).
- 2026-06-13 **OPEN BUG (not yet fixed): propose_issue RepositoryRef = slug not CR name.** internal/restapi/handlers.go proposeIssue sets `ProposedIssue.RepositoryRef = req.RepositoryRef` from the agent, which passes "szymonrychu/<repo>" (slug). createProposal does `r.Get(client.ObjectKey{Name: RepositoryRef})` -> Repository CRs are named "<repo>" (no slash, k8s-valid) -> always 404. FIX OPTIONS: resolve RepositoryRef by matching the slug to a Repository by URL/OwnerRepo, or have the brainstorm/propose_issue path pass the CR name. Blocks all brainstorm idea creation; brainstorm stays disabled until fixed.
- 2026-06-13 **Operator deploy path is currently set-image, NOT clean helm.** helm release v42 (chart 0.0.0-7d45bd9) FAILED on server-side-apply field-ownership conflicts: Deployment `.image` owned by "kubectl-set" (prior manual image pins / the drift) and Secret tatara-scm `.data.token` owned by "kubectl-patch" (bot PAT patched 2026-06-13T06:16Z, AFTER sops's last apply -> live token is almost certainly NEWER than sops). helmfile uses `--server-side`; forcing (`--force-conflicts`) would overwrite the token with the stale sops value and break the bot. So label-lifecycle (7d45bd9) and the cap (4dffcc2) were deployed via `kubectl set image` (urgent buildx -> harbor containers/tatara-operator:<sha>, then set-image + scale). Infra MR !1108 bumped chart 0.0.0-7d45bd9 + image tag but the helm APPLY child-pipeline fails on these conflicts (job reports success; helm release is `failed`). CLEAN apply is BLOCKED until the tatara-scm token is reconciled: confirm sops scmToken == live PAT (or re-encrypt the current PAT into default.secrets.yaml), then a force-apply is safe and re-establishes helm ownership of image+token. New ScmSpec label fields (ideaLabel/approvedLabel/rejectedLabel) won't reach the cluster CRD until that clean apply, but the operator defaults them in code so function is unaffected.

- 2026-06-13 (#16, NOT yet deployed) Resolved the M5 deferred follow-up "GitLab Comment targets /issues/{iid}/notes". GitHub/GitLab parity bug: `scm.GitLab.Comment` parsed only the `#` separator (`glHashRef`) and unconditionally posted to `/projects/{p}/issues/{iid}/notes`, so every write-back aimed at a GitLab MR either errored (`!` ref -> malformed) or silently hit a different *issue* with the same iid. FIX: `Comment` now routes on the ref separator - a `!` ref goes through the existing `mrNote` (`/merge_requests/{iid}/notes`, previously ClosePR-only), a `#` ref keeps the issues path; added `glBangRef` mirroring `glHashRef`. Second defect: `createScanTask` (projectscan.go) hardcoded `#` for ALL candidates incl. MRs, so a scan-created GitLab MR Task carried `group/proj#42` (wrong object); now emits `!` when `srcCand.isPR && provider=="gitlab"`. GitHub stays on `#` for both (shared `/issues/{n}/comments` endpoint). Bot-PR-entry binder is createScanTask (covered); `recordExistingProposal` is issue-only so `#` is correct there. Tests: TestGitLabCommentMR (asserts `/merge_requests/42/notes`), TestGitLabBangRef, TestCreateScanTaskGitLabMR (`!` for MR, `#` for issue). NOTE: go toolchain absent in triage env - not locally built/tested, relying on CI.
- 2026-06-13 (48ea02b, DEPLOYED live, VERIFIED) Per-repo backpressured brainstorm + approval backstop. brainstorm() was repos[0]-only/MaxPerCycle/daily; now per-repo, hourly, capped: for each repo skip if an in-flight brainstorm Task already targets it (the live-SCM proposal count lags until the issue is opened), else count open non-PR issues bearing spec.scm.approvalLabel via ListOpenIssues; if >= maxOpenProposals (new BrainstormActivity CRD field, default 3) skip, else create exactly ONE brainstorm Task -> saturates to 3 over ~3h then pauses. New approvalBackstop() (every scan cycle) recovers a missed approval webhook: for each AwaitingApproval proposal Task it reads the issue and, if approved (label removed) + open + no impl running for that issue + ApprovalRequired + bot-authored, flips ConditionApprovalApproved (RetryOnConflict) mirroring the webhook's flipApproval guard. KEY BUG caught in review: the backstop first matched the issue by Source.Number, but createProposal records Source.Number=0 (real number lives only in Source.IssueRef) -> it was a production no-op masked by a test that fabricated Number; fixed to match Source.IssueRef (build "owner/repo#n" from the listing). Identity signal: an OPEN issue carrying approvalLabel == an agent proposal awaiting approval (operator applies the label on proposal creation; human removes it to approve), so no author lookup needed for the cap. Config (infra MR !1102): brainstorm.schedule 0 6 -> 0 * * * * hourly, maxOpenProposals 3; Project CRD re-applied (kubectl, Helm skips crds/). VERIFIED live: brainstorm fired picked=6 (one per repo, all under cap); boot-race requeue fired on the fresh brainstorm pods too. Spec/plan docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-13-brainstorm-backpressure-approval-backstop*.
- 2026-06-13 (b0d0554, DEPLOYED live, VERIFIED) Triage agent was BLIND to the issue. `lifecycleTriageText` rendered `task.Spec.Goal` (the operator string "Triage issue owner/repo#N") into the prompt's "Issue body:" and showed no title -> the agent saw its own goal as the body and triaged every issue as empty (observed: on a fully-populated issue it commented "the title is empty and the body only contains the placeholder text 'Triage issue ...'"). Root cause: no issue-content reader (SCMReader had ListIssueComments but no GetIssue) + the scanned body is dropped at Task creation, so the prompt fell back to the goal. FIX: add `scm.IssueContent` + `SCMReader.GetIssue` (GitHub GET /repos/{o}/{r}/issues/{n} json "body"; GitLab GET /projects/{esc}/issues/{n} json "description"); `buildTriagePromptFor` now live-fetches title+body (comments already were) and renders them, never falling back to the goal. VERIFIED live: re-triggered tatara-cli#21 (comment reactivation) -> agent flipped Triage->Implement (was `discuss`/"no actionable content") = it now sees the real bug. Branch fix/triage-sees-issue.
- 2026-06-13 (b0d0554, DEPLOYED live, VERIFIED) Agent-pod BOOT-RACE no longer backs off. A turn submit to a wrapper whose turn server is still booting (pod Ready + Service endpoints up, listener/route not yet) returned a transport error to Reconcile -> controller-runtime EXPONENTIAL backoff -> task idled at Planning for minutes; amplified by resetAgentRun (fresh pod per lifecycle state). FIX: agent classifies transport failures from http.Client.Do as `*agent.UnreachableError` (`isUnreachable` EXCLUDES timeouts + context-cancel, which keep erroring -> a hung server still backs off, not loops); `handleAgentUnreachable` requeues those on a 5s FIXED interval (no backoff) at BOTH submit sites, and BOUNDS a never-reachable pod via `annAgentUnreachableSince`+`agentBootDeadline=5m` -> terminate Failed/AgentUnreachable; marker cleared on successful submit (recordTurn) + on resetAgentRun. New counter `operator_agent_boot_race_requeue_total` (the requeue is now reconcile-success, else invisible). VERIFIED live: the Implement-state fresh pod for tatara-cli#21 logged `"agent not yet accepting turns; requeuing" requeueAfter:"5s"` x2 then converged (turn submitted, marker cleared). Branch fix/agent-boot-race-requeue. Both fixes opus-reviewed (2 rounds for boot-race: classification-breadth + terminal-bound closed).
- 2026-06-13 (43be6f3, DEPLOYED live) Lifecycle conflict-spin FIX + deploy. Live bug found during in-cluster verification: the implement handler writes status (the iteration counter) just before `driveAgentRun`'s plain `Phase="Planning"` Status().Update; the cached client lags the API server so that Update raced/errored the reconcile, the retry re-entered at Phase=="" and re-ran the increment, spinning `lifecycleIterations` 0->10 in ~2s -> EVERY lifecycle task Parked at iter=10/turns=0 (agents never ran a turn; Triage even reached implement with turns=0). Fix: RetryOnConflict the Planning-phase set in `driveAgentRun` (absorb the conflict internally so the reconcile does not error->retry->re-increment). LESSON: a cached re-read after a write does NOT cure the conflict (cache is stale right after the write); use RetryOnConflict or an uncached read. Deployed live: operator c274cc9 then 43be6f3, cli 8978d4d, wrapper a7eab9b - images BUILT LOCALLY via buildx (CI was broken, see global memory arc-runners-die-on-job-pickup). 405-loop death verified live.
- 2026-06-13 (issue-lifecycle, on main 6976ae7, NOT yet deployed) New `issueLifecycle` Task kind: one persistent Task per issue carries an S1..S7 state machine in `Status.LifecycleState` (Triage->Conversation->Implement->MRCI->Merge->MainCI->Done/Stopped/Parked), reusing the existing agent-run + writeback primitives. Binders (issueScan/mrScan/webhook) now create `issueLifecycle` (Triage for issues, MRCI-entry for bot PRs) instead of `triageIssue`/`selfImprove`; legacy arms retained reachable for in-flight migration. KILLS the live `selfImprove ... merge -> 405 merge conflicts` controller-runtime backoff loop: the Merge state classifies a 405 as a conflict and spawns a resolve-conflict re-implement turn returning nil error (regression test `TestLifecycleMerge_405ConflictSpawnsResolveAttempt_ErrNil`); the legacy `writeBackSelfImprove` 405 path was ALSO guarded (absorb->clearWritebackPending) so the loop dies for any in-flight selfImprove Task without a manual deploy step. Spec/plans `docs/superpowers/{specs/2026-06-12-issue-lifecycle-agent-design.md,plans/2026-06-12-issue-lifecycle-m*}` (parent tatara repo).
- 2026-06-13 (issue-lifecycle) M3 context-guard ADAPTATION: the literal spec "context >50% after S7" does NOT map, because each implement run spawns a FRESH wrapper pod (resetAgentRun tears it down at run-end) - no long-lived session accumulates context across S4-S7 iterations. Realized as operator-computed-at-transitions: at each failure transition into Implement (MRCI/Merge-405/MainCI fail) the operator computes `LastTurnInputTokens*100/contextWindowTokens`; >= handoverThresholdPercent (default 50) marks `tatara.dev/pending-handover-resume` + populates `Status.Handover` (agent's `submit_handover` doc, else operator-built from ResultSummary+ImplementContext+branch); the next fresh Implement run injects it. Wrapper needs NO change (its `turn.Record.usage` was already posted to the callback; operator just deserializes it). Still honors the "operator-computed from usage" decision.
- 2026-06-13 TOOLING GAP: `golangci-lint run` here does NOT gofmt-check (no gofmt/gofumpt linter enabled), so unformatted files pass lint + pre-commit and only `gofmt -l .` catches them. Always run `gofmt -l .` separately before merge (two M3/M4 files slipped through committed-dirty).
- 2026-06-12 (0.4.2) Ingest Job hardening: TTL lowered 3600->600s (self-GC 10m after finish); operator exponential backoff (30s base, 2x per failure, 30m cap) prevents re-creation flood - previously a failing Job was re-created on every reconcile with no gate, causing a 449-pod/144-job incident; IngestFailureCount + LastIngestFailureTime added to RepositoryStatus for state tracking.
- 2026-06-09 (0.2.12) B2 fix: `MemoryNotReady` was only cleared on the ingest path (after the `if !want` early-return), so already-ingested repos kept the stale condition. Reordered the memory gate + clear before `ingestDecision`, persisting the flip immediately. Also: the agent's headless CLI auth fails `unauthorized_client` because `tatara-cli-oidc/client-secret` (sops `cliOidcClientSecret`) is STALE vs the live tatara-operator Keycloak client; the operator's own `OPERATOR_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET` works - user must re-sync the sops value (NOT a code bug).
- 2026-06-09 (0.2.11) Agent-native tatara tools (operator half): inject
  `TATARA_OPERATOR_URL` (config `OPERATOR_URL`, default
  `http://tatara-operator.tatara.svc:8080`) onto the agent pod so the CLI's
  `task_*`/`subtask_*` MCP tools have an endpoint - previously only
  `TATARA_MEMORY_URL` was set. Also clear the stale `MemoryNotReady` condition on
  Repositories once `project.status.memory.phase == Ready` (it lingered from
  provisioning and misled - the agent self-audit flagged it). Pairs with
  tatara-cli headless client-credentials auth + wrapper `tatara mcp-config` at
  boot. Note: the operator subagent died mid-O1 (socket error); O1 was finished +
  O2 done by the integrator.
- 2026-06-09 (0.2.10) Report/question tasks were invisible: a task with no code
  change pushes no branch, so write-back opened no PR and returned WITHOUT
  commenting - the agent's answer (`status.ResultSummary`) never reached the
  issue. Now when zero PRs are opened, `doWriteBack` posts the ResultSummary (or
  Goal) as an issue comment, so "check X / report Y" issues surface their result.
  Found via a user create-then-label issue ("check all MCP tools, report a table")
  that ran but produced nothing visible.
- 2026-06-09 (0.2.9) Cross-repo agent tasks: BuildPod gains a repos param and sets TATARA_REPOS (JSON array, primary first) so the wrapper clones all Project repos into /workspace/<name>; planTurnText gains the multi-repo workspace layout instruction; doWriteBack loops over all Project repos and attempts OpenChange per repo (4xx/no-branch repos are skipped), collects all PR URLs, and comments the issue with all links; primary PR URL remains in task.Status.PrURL. fakeWriterPerRepo added to writeback tests. Boy-scout: gofmt trailing blank line in operator_metrics.go.
- 2026-06-09 (0.2.8) Dedupe Task creation by issue ref: creating an issue WITH
  the label fires both `issues.opened` (label present) and `issues.labeled`, so
  the webhook made TWO Tasks (two agents, two PRs) per issue. `handleWorkItem`
  now skips creation if a non-terminal Task already exists for the issue ref;
  re-labeling after completion still re-triggers. Fixes the dogfood "create an
  issue with the label -> 2 agents" gotcha.
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.7) Agent created ZERO subtasks so nothing got implemented: the
  plan-turn prompt (`turnloop.planTurnText`) said "Do not start implementation in
  this turn", only `subtask_create`. Agents didn't decompose, so the loop ended
  with no work and write-back had no diff. Relaxed the prompt: implement small
  tasks directly in the plan turn (the wrapper auto-commits/pushes each turn),
  decompose into Subtasks only for multi-step work. Pairs with wrapper 0.1.3
  (excludes wrapper session config from the commit; bakes superpowers skills).
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.6) Agent never produced a PR: the agent edits files but does
  not reliably branch/commit/push, so write-back hit `422 head invalid` (branch
  `tatara/task-*` never existed). Fix split across repos: wrapper now enforces
  the git workflow (checkout -b + commit/push per turn, 0.1.2); operator exports
  `agent.TaskBranch` (single source of the `tatara/task-<name>` convention) and
  passes it as `TASK_BRANCH` env to the agent pod. `controller.taskBranch` now
  delegates to `agent.TaskBranch` so write-back, turn prompts, and the wrapper
  agree on the exact branch. Also noted this run: the operator created TWO Tasks
  for one issue (issue.opened-with-label + issue.labeled both fire) - dedupe by
  issue ref is a TODO (see ROADMAP).
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.5) lightrag `OPENAI_API_KEY` bug: `lightragEnv`
  (`internal/memory/lightrag.go`) set `LLM_BINDING_API_KEY` but not
  `OPENAI_API_KEY`. LightRAG's openai LLM/embedding paths fall back to the raw
  `OPENAI_API_KEY` env, so its async processing pipeline failed
  `KeyError 'OPENAI_API_KEY'` and every queued doc went `doc_status=failed`
  (963 on the cluster) - chunks/entities/relations never materialized even though
  `/documents/text` returned 200. Fix: source `OPENAI_API_KEY` from the same
  openai Secret (key `LLM_BINDING_API_KEY`). Surfaced only once the upstream
  chunk path drained end-to-end (memory 0.2.2/0.2.3 + ingester 0.2.2 kubectl
  fixes) - the dogfood was the first real exercise of LightRAG doc processing;
  earlier "index built" was schema/HNSW init, not document processing.
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.4) imagePullSecrets bug: operator-spawned **ingest Jobs**
  (`internal/ingest/job.go`) and **agent wrapper Pods** (`internal/agent/pod.go`)
  set their container image from a private Harbor registry but had no
  `imagePullSecrets`, so both failed `ErrImagePull` ("no basic auth credentials").
  The 0.2.1 fix only covered the per-Project memory pods (`internal/memory`).
  Fixed by mirroring the memory `imagePullSecrets(cfg)` helper in both packages,
  fed from `IMAGE_PULL_SECRET` (regcred) via `wire.go`. Surfaced only on the first
  real dogfood ingest (envtest never pulls images). Rule: every operator-spawned
  workload that pulls a private image needs imagePullSecrets wired from config.
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.3) All four cluster-managed secrets (`tatara-anthropic`,
  `tatara-cli-oidc`, `lightrag-openai`, `tatara-scm`) are now chart-rendered from
  sops values (`templates/managed-secrets.yaml`), replacing manual `kubectl create
  secret`. Each gated on its value(s); paired creds guarded with `and` so a
  half-set pair never renders an empty credential. Names from existing
  `*SecretName` values (+ new `scmSecretName`); data keys fixed by consumer code.
  Chart-only change (no Go): chart `version` 0.2.3, `appVersion` stays 0.2.2, image
  not rebuilt. Migration was delete-then-apply (manual secrets were not helm-owned,
  so adoption would conflict). Deferred: multi-project SCM secrets - would need a
  projects map (a list in `values.yaml`), which hard rule 6 forbids; one
  `tatara-scm` is rendered for the single Project.
- 2026-06-08 (0.2.2) Spawned agent auth switched from console API key to a
  long-lived Claude Code OAuth token: `pod.go` BuildPod now injects
  `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` from Secret `<anthropicSecretName>` data key
  `oauth-token` (was `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`/`api-key`). Pure replace, not additive:
  claude auth precedence puts `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` above the OAuth token, so
  injecting both would leave the OAuth token inert. Wrapper needs no change
  (it passes os.Environ() straight to the claude child; OAuth login does not
  trigger the API-key dialog claudejson.go seeds). The `tatara-anthropic`
  Secret must carry `oauth-token` (from `claude setup-token`); the old `api-key`
  key is no longer read. Drives subscription billing instead of console API.

  `~/Documents/tatara/docs/superpowers/plans/_tatara-operator-shared-contracts.md`.

- 2026-06-06 obs/auth mirror tatara-chat `internal/{obs,auth}`;

  tatara-memory-repo-ingester `internal/push/auth.go` (Keycloak `audience`

  internal/{config,obs,auth}, no-reconciler manager, Dockerfile, Makefile
  (generate/manifests/test/lint/build/image), chart skeleton lint-clean with

  `helm lint charts/tatara-operator` clean.
- 2026-06-06 scheme.Builder (sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/scheme) avoided;
  using runtime.NewSchemeBuilder + explicit AddKnownTypes per apimachinery

- 2026-06-06 Dockerfile GO_VERSION set to 1.26 (not 1.25 as in plan template)
  because go.mod requires go 1.26.0 - local toolchain is 1.26.0 per rule 1.
- 2026-06-06 Makefile HELM_BIN grep uses 'mise/installs/helm/' (trailing slash)
  to avoid matching 'helmfile' in the same mise PATH entries.
- 2026-06-06 (M1) Ingest result SHA flows via a per-Repository ConfigMap
  `<repo>-ingest-result` (key `sha`). The ingest Job patches it via the
  in-cluster API after `git rev-parse HEAD`; the reconciler pre-creates it
  (owner-ref Repository) and reads it on Job success. Chosen over pod-log
  parsing (brittle) and Job annotations (Job cannot patch itself cleanly).
  REQUIRES M6 chart to create ServiceAccount `tatara-ingest` + a Role granting
  get/create/update/patch on ConfigMaps in ns `tatara`. Ingest container also
  needs `kubectl` on PATH (the ingester image carries the Go toolchain; verify
  kubectl presence in M6, else switch the patch step to a tiny `curl` against
  the API or a dedicated sidecar).
- 2026-06-06 (M1) Re-ingest trigger: full when status.lastIngestedCommit=="",
  incremental (--since lastIngestedCommit) when annotation
  tatara.dev/reingest-requested (RFC3339) is newer than status.lastIngestTime.
  status.jobName is the single-flight guard. Conditions: Project `Ready`,
  Repository `Ingested`.
- 2026-06-06 (M1) Clone uses x-access-token:${SCM_TOKEN}@<host/path> in the
  init container; works for both GitHub and GitLab HTTPS with the Secret key
  `token`.
- 2026-06-06 (M1) OperatorMetrics registered against ctrlmetrics.Registry
  (controller-runtime's own prometheus registry) so they are served on the
  existing /metrics endpoint without a second registry or server.
- 2026-06-06 (M1) config.Config gained Namespace field (NAMESPACE env var,
  default tatara); needed by ingestConfig to namespace the Job and result CM.
- 2026-06-06 (M1) On ingest Job failure, RepositoryReconciler sets phase=Failed and clears jobName but does NOT bump lastIngestTime; an incremental failure (annotation still newer than lastIngestTime) therefore relaunches on the next reconcile. In-Job retries are bounded by the Job's backoffLimit=2; there is no separate reconciler-level backoff. This is intended.
- 2026-06-06 obs.Metrics (metrics.go) was dead code: created its own registry, never called in production. Consolidated all 5 platform metrics into OperatorMetrics (operator_metrics.go) on the injected registerer; deleted metrics.go and metrics_test.go.
- 2026-06-06 (M2) ReingestRequestedAnnotation moved to api/v1alpha1/annotations.go (canonical); internal/controller aliases it. This is the single source of truth per the plan's boy-scout correction requirement.
- 2026-06-06 (M2) SameRemote normalizes by stripping trailing .git and trailing /, then lowercasing the host for a case-insensitive compare. Chosen to handle the common GitHub/GitLab variant where clone URLs end in .git in the CRD but not in webhook payloads (or vice versa).
- 2026-06-06 (M2) Webhook server uses obs.OperatorMetrics.WebhookEvent(provider,kind,result) method (all-private fields) not a public CounterVec; plan's Task 5 code referenced a non-existent .WebhookEvents public field - corrected at implementation time.
- 2026-06-06 (M2) Work-item path (issue/mr with triggerLabel) is a metered 202 stub in M2; Task creation is deliberately deferred to M5 alongside scm.OpenChange/Comment write-back.
- 2026-06-06 (M3 Tasks 1-4) internal/restapi scaffold complete. API-drift corrections vs plan: (1) TaskStatus field is PrURL (not PRURL); (2) RepositoryStatus.LastIngestTime is *metav1.Time (pointer), nil-checked before formatting; (3) no auth.Middleware function exists - Mount takes func(http.Handler) http.Handler directly, which is the correct pattern. In-memory projectRef filtering for repositories/tasks (no fake-client field index). Branch feat/m3-restapi.
- 2026-06-06 (M3 Tasks 5-8) REST API complete. PATCH /tasks/{t} records resultSummary + dedupes AgentNote condition via apimeta.SetStatusCondition (bounded list, repeated notes update the same condition). POST /tasks/{t}/subtasks uses fmt.Sprintf("%s-st-%d", task, UnixNano) for deterministic names (fake client ignores generateName). PATCH /subtasks/{s} sets phase/result/turnId via status subresource. Shared HTTP_ADDR listener: webhook.Server gained Mount(chi.Router); webhook.HandlerRunnable added to serve arbitrary http.Handler; auth.Middleware added (mirrors tatara-chat); addWebhookServer in wire.go now takes ctx for OIDC discovery and builds one chi.Mux with both route groups. M3 Part A merged to main.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 Tasks 6-8) Turn-loop annotation contract: in-flight turnId stored as annotation `tatara.dev/current-turn` on Task; executing Subtask name as `tatara.dev/current-subtask`; callback requeues by bumping `tatara.dev/turn-complete` (RFC3339); pod recreation count in `tatara.dev/pod-recreations`. Annotations used (not new status fields) to avoid reopening the M0 CRD schema.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 Tasks 6-8) M5 write-back hook: terminated Succeeded Tasks get condition `type=WritebackPending, status=True, reason=AwaitingM5`. M5 SCM path queries this condition and clears it once the PR/MR + comment is landed.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 Tasks 6-8) API-drift: plan used `meta.SetStatusCondition` from `k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta` but the actual import path is `k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/api/meta` as `apimeta`; resolved at implementation. `mkProject`/`mkRepository` helpers in task_controller_test.go renamed to `mkTaskProject`/`mkTaskRepository` to avoid collision with pre-existing helpers in repository_controller_test.go (different signature). `contains`/`indexOf` helpers already declared in repository_controller_test.go; removed from task test file.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 Tasks 6-8) TurnsCompleted increment: bumped in `recordTurn` when the incoming task has a non-empty `annTurnComplete`, i.e. one turn per delivered callback. Plan turn (turn 0) completes without incrementing until the next recordTurn sees the callback; subtask turns each increment once on close.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 Task 9) Turn<->Task correlation via annotations (tatara.dev/current-turn, current-subtask, turn-complete, pod-recreations), not new CRD status fields, to avoid re-opening the M0 schema. M5 write-back keys off the WritebackPending condition set on Succeeded. CallbackServer.Session is optional (nil-guarded in poll backstop tick) so unit tests that only test the HTTP handler need not wire a Session.
- 2026-06-06 (M5) Agent branch naming convention: `tatara/task-<task-name>` (deterministic, no status annotation needed). TaskReconciler.SCMFor factory (injected, faked in tests) resolves provider from Task.Spec.Source.Provider or derived from Repository remote URL host (gitlab.* -> gitlab, else github). Task.Spec.Source is *TaskSource (pointer); nil-checked throughout. Task.Status.PrURL is the Go field (json: prURL) - plan used PRURL incorrectly; corrected at implementation. OpenChange permanent 4xx clears WritebackPending without requeue; transient errors propagate. Comment failure is non-fatal (log+continue). Webhook work-item -> Task path now complete: Task created with goal=ev.Body, source{provider,issueRef,url}, projectRef/repositoryRef, owner-ref Project. M2 stub test TestIssueWithTriggerLabelStubbed removed (replaced by workitem_test.go). GitLab Comment targets /issues/{iid}/notes not /merge_requests - appropriate for issue-origin work items; MR-origin comment targeting is follow-up if needed.
- 2026-06-06 (M5 code-review fixes) Operator<->agent branch contract: `tatara/task-<task-name>` is injected via turn prompts (planTurnText/turnText in turnloop.go); the operator also opens the PR/MR targeting that same branch. If the wrapper ever enforces branch pushing itself it MUST use this same value. The branch is NOT communicated via REPO_BRANCH env. providerForRemote now correctly handles github-substring hosts and logs a warning for unknown hosts defaulting to github. Permanent 4xx from OpenChange uses neutral reason WritebackSkipped. Issue comment body no longer duplicates the Source footer from writeBackBody.
- 2026-06-06 (M4 code-review fixes) Four distinct ports: HEALTH_ADDR (:8081) = manager health probe bind; INTERNAL_ADDR (:8082) = callback server bind (not via ingress); METRICS_ADDR (:9090); HTTP_ADDR (:8080). CALLBACK_URL (full routable in-cluster base URL) is separate from INTERNAL_ADDR (bind addr). M6 chart MUST: (1) set CALLBACK_URL to the operator callback Service DNS (e.g. http://tatara-operator-internal.tatara.svc:8082); (2) create a Service exposing INTERNAL_ADDR (:8082) for the callback path; (3) HEALTH_ADDR/INTERNAL_ADDR/METRICS_ADDR/HTTP_ADDR are four distinct ports in the Deployment. Added per-turn deadline: tatara.dev/turn-started-at stamped on each turn submit; turns exceeding turnTimeoutSeconds+60s grace -> Task phase=Failed/TurnTimeout in both the reconciler and the poll backstop. RetryOnConflict wraps Task annotation + Subtask status writes in turncallback.go. Empty/missing turnId -> HTTP 400 before any Task lookup. resolveTaskByTurn skips Tasks with empty annCurrentTurn. markSubtaskDone now uses task.Namespace (not r.PodConfig.Namespace). atoiSafe/itoa replaced by strconv.Atoi/Itoa.
- 2026-06-06 (M6) Chart hardened. Cluster-agnostic (rule 14): no baked regcred/affinity/ingress-host/storage-class; all from infra helmfile tatara bucket. Config: 15 keys via envConfig helper -> ConfigMap envFrom (rule 6); OPERATOR_OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET only secret env, via templated Secret (existingSecret override supported). Four distinct ports in Deployment: http :8080, health :8081, internal :8082, metrics :9090. Liveness/readiness probes on HEALTH_ADDR (:8081). Two Services: main (http:8080 + metrics:9090) and tatara-operator-internal (internal:8082). CALLBACK_URL in ConfigMap must be set to http://tatara-operator-internal.<ns>.svc:8082 by infra values.
- 2026-06-06 (M6) Manager RBAC: namespaced Role+RoleBinding (tatara.dev CRDs+status, batch/jobs, pods/services/configmaps/secrets-read/networkpolicies, events) + cluster-scoped ClusterRole+ClusterRoleBinding for CRD-reader (apiextensions.k8s.io/customresourcedefinitions). Single-cluster single-release assumed; ClusterRole name carries release fullname.

Egress: DNS, tatara-memory:8080, tatara-chat:8080, operator:8080+8082, HTTPS-to-any (443). CIDR tightening of 443 deferred to wrapper hardening ROADMAP.

- 2026-06-07 (N1) cnpg api module pinned to v1.29.1 (matches live operator image ghcr.io/cloudnative-pg/cloudnative-pg:1.29.1 in all namespaces); part of main cloudnative-pg module, heavy transitive deps accepted to use upstream Cluster type (hand-rolling Cluster struct would violate rule 4). go directive bumped 1.26.0->1.26.3 (cnpg requires >=1.26.3; still 1.26 minor series).

All owner-ref'd to Project; make test + lint green.

- 2026-06-07 (N1) cnpg v1.29.1 exports SchemeGroupVersion (not GroupVersion); PGCluster TypeMeta.APIVersion uses cnpgv1.SchemeGroupVersion.String(). All struct field names (Instances, StorageConfiguration.Size, Bootstrap.InitDB.{Database,Owner,PostInitApplicationSQL}) match v1.27.x plan exactly.
- 2026-06-07 (N2) ProjectReconciler.Reconcile now provisions the full per-project memory stack via SSA (client.Apply/Patch), computes status.memory.{phase,endpoint}, and sets MemoryReady condition; single status update persists both Ready+MemoryReady atomically. fakeStackHealthy must set .Status.Replicas=1 in addition to ReadyReplicas/AvailableReplicas or envtest API server rejects the update (readyReplicas<=replicas validation).
- 2026-06-07 (N2) client.Apply (Patch variant) marked //nolint:staticcheck; deprecated in controller-runtime v0.24.1 with no stated removal version. Migration to typed r.Apply(ctx, applyconfig) requires generated applyconfiguration types for all 10 stack objects (incl. cnpg Cluster) - backlog item (NOT done in N4; the nolint is an accepted rule-4 deferral with this rationale until typed-apply is feasible).
- 2026-06-07 (N2 code-review fixes) operator_memory_stacks gauge: SetMemoryStacks(phase,1) replaced by SetMemoryStackCounts(prov,ready,failed int) that LISTs all Projects and sets all three phase gauges atomically after each reconcile; projects with nil status.memory are not counted. Duration histogram: fires once only on the Provisioning->Ready transition, measured from p.CreationTimestamp.Time (wall-clock to ready, not reconcile duration). applyMemoryStack neo4jPassword param removed (was unused; password Secret is applied separately). failMemory redundant ensureMemoryStatus call removed (p.Status.Memory is always non-nil at that point).

Operator `Config.MemoryBaseURL` / `MEMORY_BASE_URL` removed (stale comments in wire.go/test also removed).
- 2026-06-07 (N4) Static tatara-memory retired; per-Project memory is operator-provisioned. Chart 0.2.0: Role adds postgresql.cnpg.io clusters(+/status), apps/deployments (WAS MISSING - N2/N3 Owns(&appsv1.Deployment{}) required it), apps/statefulsets, core/persistentvolumeclaims CRUD; secrets verbs widened to create/update/patch/delete (was read-only) for generated neo4j password + memory config Secrets. ConfigMap drops MEMORY_BASE_URL (N3), adds MEMORY_IMAGE/LIGHTRAG_IMAGE/NEO4J_IMAGE/OPENAI_SECRET_NAME. cnpg Cluster CRD in charts/tatara-operator/crds: provenance header added (v1.29.1 matching go.mod); N2 had vendored it without the header. Image pins: tatara-memory:0.2.0, lightrag v1.4.16 (tag, not digest; subchart pin), neo4j:5-community (native single-node STS, not upstream neo4j chart). Shared OpenAI secret name: lightrag-openai (ns tatara, key LLM_BINDING_API_KEY).
- 2026-06-07 (N5/fix) Deploy bug: neo4j ImagePullBackOff. Two root causes: (1) all operator-spawned workloads (neo4j StatefulSet, lightrag Deployment, tatara-memory Deployment, cnpg Cluster) lacked imagePullSecrets - harbor proxy-dockerhub requires auth. Fixed via new IMAGE_PULL_SECRET config scalar (env var -> ConfigMap -> manager -> memory.Config.ImagePullSecret -> injected into all four workload pod templates). (2) neo4j:5-community is not a valid tag; replaced with neo4j:2026.04.0 (proven pullable). Chart 0.2.1. cnpg ClusterSpec.ImagePullSecrets field is []cnpgv1.LocalObjectReference which is a type alias for github.com/cloudnative-pg/machinery/pkg/api.LocalObjectReference (NOT corev1.LocalObjectReference) - required a separate pgImagePullSecrets helper in pg.go; the corev1-typed imagePullSecrets helper in memory.go cannot be used directly.

- 2026-06-09 scm-projects: SCMWriter is the single egress interface (12 methods); GitHub board ops are net-new GraphQL (Projects v2), GitLab boards are scoped-label driven; approval label is source of truth, mirrored as ApprovalApproved condition; merge gated by MergePolicy + GetPRState CI.

2026-06-09 - **Agent-native tools were dead until cli 0.4.3.** Every wrapper agent run Succeeded with 0 subtasks / empty issue comments because tatara-cli's `tatara mcp` returned an error on tools/list (NewTool+WithRawInputSchema set both InputSchema and RawInputSchema; mcp-go refuses to marshal). claude loaded ZERO tatara tools while `claude mcp list` still said "Connected" (initialize handshake only). Root cause + fix in tatara-cli MEMORY (0.4.3, NewToolWithRawSchema). Rebuilt wrapper 0.1.8 (bundles cli 0.4.3); Project tatara agent.image -> 0.1.8 (kubectl patch; Project is kubectl-managed, not helm). Validated: bare Task -> agent called subtask_create via MCP -> Subtask created. Auth (operator/memory OIDC) was never the blocker; the curl probes bypassed claude.

2026-06-11 - **autonomous-cron ACTIVATED on Project tatara via personal PAT (no bot account, no Project board).** Cron scans iterate the Project's Repository CRs (projectReposForScan: RepositoryList filtered by Spec.ProjectRef); the GitHub Projects-v2 board is OPTIONAL (issueScan adds board items only when Spec.Scm.Board != nil). Activation gate is purely `Scm != nil && Cron != nil` - no botLogin/board/org dependency. botLogin == the PAT owner (szymonrychu), so mrScan routes the owner's own PRs to selfImprove and others' to review (author/actor egress gate via GetPRState). prReactionScope does NOT gate cron mrScan (it scans all open PRs); it only gates the reactive webhook path. First fire verified: listed 5 PRs/7 issues, created selfImprove+triageIssue+brainstorm Tasks; agent pods run wrapper 0.1.12.
2026-06-11 - **Wrapper image version numbering diverged from the repo.** Deployed agent image was 0.1.11, but wrapper repo appVersion only ever went 0.1.4->0.1.5->0.1.7->0.1.8 - the 0.1.9/0.1.10/0.1.11 tags were ad-hoc `make image VERSION=...` overrides during the graphify deploys, never committed to Chart.yaml. Resolution: re-cut wrapper 0.1.12 (forward of the deployed 0.1.11). Content is determined by the BAKED CLI, not the wrapper number: wrapper 0.1.12 bakes cli 0.6.0, a linear superset (17 graphify code_* tools + SCM propose/review/pr_outcome + issue_outcome), so it strictly supersedes 0.1.11 (older graphify-era cli) despite numbers. RULE: always cut wrapper forward of the deployed tag and verify content via the baked cli version.
2026-06-11 - **stampScan lacks RetryOnConflict (minor, 0.4.1 backlog).** projectscan.go stampScan does Get + Status().Update without retry, so a scan racing the main reconcile logs a benign "object has been modified" ERROR; the stamp succeeds on the next reconcile (timestamps ARE recorded; hourly schedule means no hot loop, dedup prevents duplicate Tasks). Wrap in retry.RetryOnConflict to kill the ERROR noise.
2026-06-11 - **Egress: applied the additive tatara-egress-internet allow ONLY (ipBlock 0.0.0.0/0:443 + DNS, keyed on tatara.io/egress=internet), NOT the runbook's namespace-wide default-deny-egress.** ns tatara today: only tatara-operator-managed-pods selects pods (managed-by=tatara-operator); chat/memory/cnpg/neo4j are selected by NO egress policy -> open egress. A blanket default-deny would newly restrict and likely break them. Manager pod is NOT managed-by -> open egress -> cron SCM calls (443) unrestricted. Hardening the namespace needs a per-pod egress audit first.
2026-06-12 (issue #3) memoryStackHealth tolerates NotFound: a NotFound read on any of the 4 owned objects (cnpg Cluster, neo4j STS, lightrag/memory Deployments) is treated as not-yet-ready (count stays 0 -> memoryPhase=Provisioning) instead of erroring. reconcileMemory no longer calls failMemory("HealthError") on a health read error: a non-NotFound (transient API/cache) error leaves phase+MemoryReady untouched and requeues with backoff. Failed is now reserved for password/apply errors only. Key insight: genuine provisioning stalls (objects exist but never become ready) already surface as a stuck Provisioning phase via memoryPhase, never Failed - so health-read-error->Failed only ever caused the spurious flap the issue describes; removing it needs no grace-period state.
2026-06-12 - **Per-repo fan-out (improvement #1): scan creation is intentionally O(#repos), not the old global maxPerCycle.** mrScan/issueScan now top up each repo's lane to MaxPerRepo (CronActivity field, was MaxPerCycle; default 1) via selectPerRepo + laneOccupancy. laneOccupancy counts a repo's Tasks in {Pending,Planning,Running} (EXCLUDES AwaitingApproval/Succeeded/Failed) so an awaiting-approval proposal does NOT hold the lane. Consequence: a single scan can create up to #repos Tasks (vs <=maxPerCycle before). This is BOUNDED, not unbounded: occupancy counts Pending, so each lane caps at MaxPerRepo and total in-flight Task CRs <= 2*#repos*MaxPerRepo (12 for tatara's 6 repos). Execution is throttled by spec.maxConcurrentTasks (atConcurrencyCap, EXECUTION-time gate on isActive only - it never throttles CREATION). A 60s backlogRequeue refills freed lanes. Rationale: a creation-side cap would defeat the fan-out; for tatara (6 repos) the bound is trivial. If a Project ever has very many repos, add a per-scan creation cap. brainstorm (BrainstormActivity.MaxPerCycle) is untouched (genuinely per-cycle). Branch feat/per-repo-fanout; awaiting deploy.
2026-06-13 - **3-label issue lifecycle (tatara-idea / tatara-approved / tatara-rejected) replaces the label-toggle approval model.** Approval is now CONVERSATION-driven: the Triage agent reads the full thread (handleIssueComment already resets a live lifecycle Task to Triage on any non-bot comment) and emits issue_outcome; the operator projects it onto exactly one managed label via setLifecycleLabel egress (internal/controller/labels.go) at each finishTriage transition: implement->approved+Implement, discuss->idea+Conversation, close->rejected+close+Done.
